# Save the agent overview for a session (Beta) - Docs by LangChain

Source: https://docs.langchain.com/langsmith/smith-api/issues-agent/save-the-agent-overview-for-a-session-beta

---

Beta: This endpoint is in active development and may change without notice.

Saves the issues agent overview content server-side, creating or updating
the backing private Prompt Hub repo and linking it to the issues agent config.

PATCH

/

api

/

v1

/

platform

/

sessions

/

{session\_id}

/

issues-agent

/

overview

Error

A valid request URL is required to generate request examples
